# Black Friday & Cyber Monday 2026 Deals, All in One List

**1254 live deals** in one place, **610 with promo codes**. Built by merging the biggest community deal lists on GitHub, removing duplicates and expired deals, then adding big-brand deals (VPNs, hosting, domains, password managers, cloud, courses) found by web research.

Black Friday is **Nov 27, 2026**, Cyber Monday is **Nov 30, 2026**. Last built: **2026-11-23**.

> Prices and codes come from the vendors' own listings and the lists below. Always check the price at checkout. "Ends" is the latest date mentioned in the deal, so treat it as a guide.

## Sources

Merged from these lists (thank you to their maintainers and contributors):

- [trungdq88/Awesome-Black-Friday-Cyber-Monday](https://github.com/trungdq88/Awesome-Black-Friday-Cyber-Monday)
- [mRs-/Black-Friday-Deals](https://github.com/mRs-/Black-Friday-Deals)
- [danmindru/rare-big-deal](https://github.com/danmindru/rare-big-deal)
- [twf-nikhila/Awesome-Black-Friday-Cyber-Monday-deals](https://github.com/twf-nikhila/Awesome-Black-Friday-Cyber-Monday-deals)
- [Pimjo/black-friday-deals](https://github.com/Pimjo/black-friday-deals)
- [KevinKaul/black-friday-deals-2026](https://github.com/KevinKaul/black-friday-deals-2026)
- Web research: vendor Black Friday pages and deal roundups, linked per deal as "web".

3039 rows parsed, 1280 unique deals after merging, 26 expired deals removed.

## Rebuild

```sh
./scripts/fetch.sh     # refresh sources/ from GitHub (needs gh)
node scripts/build.ts  # regenerate data/deals.json and README.md (Node 23.6+)
```

The full data is in [data/deals.json](data/deals.json). To add a deal, add it to `data/extra.json` and open a PR.
